pair_eval(h, fpos, fneg, p, q):
   if h[p]==1 and h[q]==0 swap.
   hp = h.copy()
   fp = fpos.copy(); fn = fneg.copy()
   flip(hp, fp, fn, p, +1)
   flip(hp, fp, fn, q, -1)
   c = max(fp.max(), fn.max())
   return c
But copy each. We can pre-allocate temporary arrays and use np.copyto to avoid allocation? Maybe not needed.

Local search algorithm:
def binary_local_search(h, fpos, fneg, time_limit, seed, iterations=None):
   best_c_count = max
   stall=0
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   for iter in ...:
      # compute top lags and scores
      select up to k=24 lags: combine fpos and fneg; if tied random. We can choose lags with values >= best_count-1 (if any) else top min(24, n//?); avoid too many if all max many.
      compute score with weights maybe softmax based on f - best_count (temp=0.5) for selected.
      zcand = indices h==0 sorted by score ascending (lowest), take top nz (e.g., 30)
      ocand = indices h==1 sorted by score descending, top no (30)
      # Try pairs from top lists, maybe all combinations capped 200. Use current h/f arrays.
      found=False
      for p in zcand:
        for q in ocand:
          if p==q continue
          c = evaluate_pair(...)
          if c < best_count - 1e-9: accept, update h, fpos, fneg; best_count=c; found=True; break
      if not found:
        # random pairs maybe 50
        sample p zero, q one, evaluate; accept if improved.
      if not found: stall++ else 0; if stall > threshold break.
   return h, fpos, fneg, best_count
But evaluating all 30*30=900 pairs per iteration with copies O(900*450) ~400k per iter; 100 iter 40M okay. But Python loop overhead high. We can limit top 12 =>144 pairs. Use continuous gradient maybe enough.

Detailed block objective:
def _softmax(x): x = x - max; e=exp; return e/e.sum()
def _objective_block_fracs(a,b,start):
   # a length p ones frac sum1, b length q zeros frac sum1.
   intervals ones/zeros depending start.
   Build runs []
   if start==1:
      if p>q: sequence [a1,b1,...,a_p]
      else p==q: [a1,b1,...,a_p,b_q]
      # cannot p<q starting 1? If alternating equal or one more ones. For m even start1 p=q. If start1 and p<q impossible for alternating first/last? start1 end0 p=q; start1 end1 p=q+1.
   if start==0:
      if q>p: [b1,a1,...,b_q]
      else p==q: [b1,a1,...,b_q,a_p]
   Compute coordinates.
   p and q pass.
   boundaries = cumulative [0, ..., total] where total = sum ones+zeros = 1+1 =2.
   Breakpoints unique sorted {abs(bi-bj) in [0,2]}
   def overlap(F, G, k): # F target 1 intervals, G 0 intervals
      total=0
      for l1,r1 in F:
         for u,v in G:
            lo=max(l1, u-k); hi=min(r1, v-k); if hi>lo total += hi-lo
      return total
   maxv=0
   for k in bps:
      if 0<=k<=2:
         val=overlap(ones, zeros, k)
         if val>maxv: maxv=val
         val=overlap(zeros, ones, k)
         if val>maxv: maxv=val
   return maxv

This continuous objective for binary step. But note for nonperiodic, shifts k can be negative; positive k with ones->zeros and zeros->ones covers both directions. Max over k≥0 enough. Good.

Pair evaluation function top-level cannot be closure. We'll create separate helper:
def pair_move_max(h, fp, fn, p, q):
   # returns new max count; does not modify input. h,fp,fn are current.
   hp = h.copy()
   fp2 = fp.copy(); fn2=fn.copy()
   if h[p]==0 and h[q]==1:
      flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, p, 1)
      flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, q, -1)
   elif h[p]==1 and h[q]==0: swap
   else return currentmax? 
   return max(fp2.max(), fn2.max())
But allocating hp every candidate expensive. We'll use in local loop preallocated temp arrays? Top-level can accept temp.

Score computation helper:
def active_scores(h, fp, fn, n, k_lags=24, k_candidates=24, seed=None):
   maxf = max(fp.max(), fn.max())
   # Build list of (d, type, w)
   # Candidate lags: indices d with values. We can find top k by concatenating fp,fn? Use np.argsort? Length n small. 
   # To avoid many max lags, select top k_lags from combined values; weight = exp((val - maxf)/1.0) maybe.
   vals = np.concatenate((fp, fn)); indices...
We can use np.argpartition for top k. But if many max, argpartition picks arbitrary; good.
   top_idx = np.argpartition(vals, -k)[-k:]
   # ensure weights: for val, w = exp((val - maxf)/temp) with temp=0.5 or 1.0. For val=maxf weight1, maxf-1 0.37 if temp1; if many max, okay.
   # Accumulate score = zeros(n)
   for idx in top_idx:
      d = idx % (n-1); if idx < n-1 type=0 else type=1.
      val = vals[idx]; w=exp((val - maxf)/0.5) if val>=maxf else 0? For val max-1 small. Could just w=1 for top values to focus.

def apply_pair_swap(h, fp, fn, p, q):

Implement smooth_gradient(x, alpha):
   corr = np.correlate(x,1-x)
   m = max(corr)
   y = alpha*(corr-m); clip; e=exp; w=e/sum
   g=np.zeros(n)
   center=n-1
   for idx,t in enumerate(np.nonzero(w>1e-8)):
       l = center - int(t)
       wgt=w[idx]
       add_grad_lag(g, x, l, wgt)
   return dx*g

For binary A on [0,n], f_pos(d)=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} A_i (1-A_{i+d}). Express in terms of prefix sums? f_pos(d) = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} A_i - ∑ A_i A_{i+d}. L = p (left positions: p-d for d=1..L)
If raise 0->1:
  fpos: p as first (i=p) with right zero: if h[p+d]==0 add. fpos[:R] += 1 - h[p+1:]
  p as second (i=p-d) with left one? fpos counts h_i=1, h_{i+d}=0. If p becomes 1, then pair i=p-d previously h_i=1, h_p=0 counted; now h_p=1 not counted: subtract if h[p-d]==1. fpos[:L] -= h[p-1::-1]

  p as second (h_{i+d}=1 now true) with left zero: add if h[p-d]==0: fneg[:L] += 1 - h[p-1::-1]

  fneg:
  p as first: h_i=0 condition after true with right one: add h[p+1:]

11. pair_max_count(h, fp, fn, p, q): returns max after swap or current? We'll implement copies and flip. But for speed in local search, maybe inline with temp arrays.
Top-level function:
def pair_try(h, fp, fn, p, q):
   # assumes h[p]!=h[q]
   hp = h.copy(); fp2=fp.copy(); fn2=fn.copy()
   if hp[p]==1 and hp[q]==0:
       flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, q, 1) # q zero to one
       flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, p, -1) # p one to zero
   else:
       flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, p, 1)
       flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, q, -1)
   return int(max(fp2.max(), fn2.max()))
But if p zero q one, first flip p then q. Good.
For apply after successful pair, we'll call similar on current arrays in place.

Discrete allocation:
def allocate_counts(fr, total):
   fr=np.asarray(fr,float); fr=np.nan_to_num(fr).clip(min=0); s=fr.sum(); if s<=0: fr=np.ones_like(fr)/fr.size else fr/=s
   raw=fr*total
   base=np.floor(raw).astype(int)
   rem=raw-base
   diff=int(total - base.sum())
   if diff>0:
      idx=np.argsort(-rem)
      for i in range(diff): base[idx[i%base.size]] +=1
   elif diff<0:
      idx=np.argsort(rem)  # remove smallest remainder, but ensure >0 maybe
      remove= -diff
      for i in range(remove):
         j=idx[i%base.size]
         if base[j] > 0: base[j]-=1
      # if some zero caused diff? recompute and add to last
      diff2=int(total - base.sum())
      if diff2>0: add to indices largest
   return base

function block_h(n, start, a, b):

   ones_counts=allocate_counts(a,n//2) # assume n even
   zeros_counts=allocate_counts(b,n//2)
   # if n odd target floor? We'll use even n for binary. For continuous, n maybe 450 even.
   runs=[]
   if start==1:

         # pattern 1,0,1,0,...,1,0 length p+q even
      elif p==q+1:
         for i in range(q): runs.append((1,ones_counts[i])); runs.append((0,zeros_counts[i])); runs.append((1,ones_counts[q]))
      else: # fallback adjust
   if start==0 similar.
   h=np.zeros(n,int8); pos=0; for val,cnt in runs: h[pos:pos+cnt]=val; pos+=cnt; if pos>n truncate; if pos<n fill? Ensure total n by allocate sums.
   return h

def continuous_hill(h0, time_limit, max_iter=2000, top_k=24, seed=0):
   n=... target=...; h=project_density(h0,target); best=...
   stall=0
   for it:
      corr = np.correlate(h,1-h); center; select top top_k lags. Weights maybe softmax with temp 0.05? For max subgradient, choose lags with c >= max - 1e-4 (maybe several). For direction, use top k values.
      g=zeros; add_grad_lag for selected with weights (maybe uniform or softmax). Use exact vals.
      d = -g; d -= mean(d)
      norm; if small break
      d/=norm
      improved=False
      for step in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8):
          cand=project_density(h+step*d,target); c=eval
          if c < h_c - 1e-12: accept; h_c=c; improved=True; break
      if not improved: try smaller (0.005)
      if not: stall++ else 0; if stall>50 break
      if c<best update
   return best

Correct add_grad_lag implementation:
def add_corr_grad(g, h, l, w):
   n=h.size; d=abs(l)
   if d==0:
      g += w*(1-2*h)
   elif l>0:
      # c_l = ∑ i=0..n-1-d h_i(1-h_{i+d})
      g[:n-d] += w*(1 - h[d:])
      g[d:] -= w*h[:-d]
   else:
      # l=-d
      g[d:] += w*(1 - h[:-d])
      g[:n-d] -= w*h[d:]
Check with l>0 example h=[1,1,0], d=1: g[:2]+=1-h[1:]=[0,1] -> [0,1,0]; g[1:]-=h[:2]=[1,1] -> [-1? g[1]=0, g[2]=-1]. Result [-1,0,-1]? Wait expected [0,0,-1]? Let's recalc: h=[1,1,0]. For l=1, c = i=0 h0(1-h1)=1*0=0; i=1 h1(1-h2)=1*1=1. Derivative wrt h0: 1-h1 =0. My formula g[:2] for i=0: 1-h1=0 good; i=1: 1-h2=1. So g[:2] adds [0,1]. Then g[d:] -= h[:-d]: g[1]-=h0=1 ->0; g[2]-=h1=1 -> -1. Result [0,0,-1]. I mistakenly set g initial [0,1,0], then after subtract g[1]=1-1=0, g[2]=-1. Correct. Good.
For l<0 h=[1,1,0], d=1: c=sum i=1..2 h_i(1-h_{i-1}) = i1 h1(1-h0)=0; i2 h2(1-h1)=0; derivative wrt h0: -h1? i2 second term? formula: g[d:] +=1-h[:-d] -> g[1]+=1-h0=0, g[2]+=1-h1=0; g[:2]-=h[d:] -> g0-=h1=-1, g1-=h2=0. Result [-1,0,0]. Check c as function h: terms h1(1-h0)+h2(1-h1). derivative h0=-h1=-1; h1=1-h0 -h2? wait d h1: h1 term derivative h1? term1 h1(1-h0) derivative wrt h1=(1-h0)=0; term2 h2(1-h1) derivative=-h2=0 =>0; h2:1-h1=0. Good.

For smooth objective weights and numpy correlate orientation: c index idx, l=center-idx. If l>0, use add_corr_grad positive. Good.

Let's detail block optimization wrapper:
_GLOBAL_BLOCK = None
def _block_wrapper(x):
    p,q,start = _GLOBAL_BLOCK
    if p is None: return 1e6
    a = _softmax(x[:p])
    b = _softmax(x[p:p+q])
    return _block_obj_fracs(a,b,start)

def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0: return np.array([1.0])
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = x - np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(x, -745, 709))
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones_like(x)/x.size
    return e/s

def _block_obj_fracs(a,b,start):
   p=a.size; q=b.size
   a=np.asarray(a,float); b=...
   if p==0 or q==0: return 0.5 maybe.
   a = a/sum; b=b/sum
   pos=0.0; ones=[]; zeros=[]; bounds=[0.0]
   val=start
   for i in range(p+q):
      if val==1:
         length=float(a[i]); l=pos; r=pos+length; ones.append((l,r)); 
      else:
         length=float(b[i]); zeros.append((l,r))
      pos += length; bounds.append(pos)
      val ^= 1
   # pos may be 2.
   # breakpoints
   br=set([0.0,2.0])
   for i in range(len(bounds)):
      for j in range(i+1,len(bounds)):
         d=abs(bounds[i]-bounds[j]); if d<=2.0+1e-12: br.add(min(2.0,d))
   maxv=0.0
   for k in br:
      if k< -1e-12 or k>2+1e-12: continue
      v1=_interval_overlap(ones, zeros, k)
      if v1>maxv: maxv=v1
      v0=_interval_overlap(zeros, ones, k)
      if v0>maxv: maxv=v0
   return float(maxv)

def _interval_overlap(I,J,k):
   s=0.0
   for l1,r1 in I:
      for l0,r0 in J:
         lo=max(l1, l0-k); hi=min(r1, r0-k)
         if hi>lo: s += hi-lo
   return s

Potential: For k=0, overlap intervals disjoint (if no overlap) gives 0. Good.

Block optimize:
def optimize_block_patterns(time_limit, seed=42):
  global _GLOBAL_BLOCK
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  results=[]
  start = time.perf_counter(); dl=start+time_limit
  for m in range(3, 9):
    for st in (1,0):
      p = (m+1)//2 if st==1 else m//2
      q = m-p
      if p<1 or q<1: continue
      dim = (p-1)+(q-1)
      starts=4 if dim<=3 else 2
      best_val=1e9; best_x=None
      x0 = rng.normal(0,1,p+q)
      for s in range(starts):
         if s==0: x0 = np.zeros(p+q)
         else: x0 = rng.normal(0,1,p+q)
         _GLOBAL_BLOCK=(p,q,st)
         try:
            res = minimize(_block_wrapper, x0, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':250, 'maxfev':500, 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-8, 'adaptive':True})
            if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
               val=_block_wrapper(res.x)
               if val<best_val: best_val=val; best_x=res.x.copy()
         except Exception: pass
         if time.perf_counter() > dl - 10: break
      if best_x is not None:
         a=_softmax(best_x[:p]); b=_softmax(best_x[p:p+q])
         results.append((best_val,p,q,st,a,b))
      if time.perf_counter() > dl-2: break
  return results

minimize with Nelder-Mead may use top-level wrapper; _GLOBAL_BLOCK set. OK.

def make_block_candidate(n, st, a, b):
   if n%2 !=0: n=n+1
   target=n//2
   ones_counts=allocate_counts(a,target)
   zeros_counts=allocate_counts(b,target)
   h=np.zeros(n,np.int8); pos=0; val=st; i1=0;i0=0
   m=a.size+b.size
   for r in range(m):
      if val==1:
         c=int(ones_counts[i1]); i1+=1
      else:
         c=int(zeros_counts[i0]); i0+=1
      if c>0:
         h[pos:pos+c]=val; pos+=c
      val^=1
   if pos<n: # adjust
      # if any counts omitted due to pos? fill with start? Better if diff.
      diff=target - int(h.sum())
      if diff !=0:
         # flip closest? Simpler: set zeros/ones to adjust
         if diff>0:
            idx=np.where(h==0)[0]
            h[idx[:diff]]=1
         else:
            idx=np.where(h==1)[0]
            h[idx[: -diff]]=0
   return h

def binary_from_fractional(hs):
   h=np.asarray(hs,float); n=h.size
   if n%2: n-1? If n odd, target impossible integer. We can resample to even: if n%2: h=upsample_h(h, n+1)

   # choose top target values
   idx=np.argsort(h, kind='mergesort')
   h2=np.zeros(n,np.int8)
   h2[idx[target:]]=1
   return h2

compute_f_arrays via bitset:
def compute_f_arrays(h):
   n=h.size
   A=0
   nz=np.nonzero(h)[0]
   for i in nz: A |= 1 << int(i)
   mask_all=(1<<n)-1
   Z=mask_all ^ A
   fp=np.zeros(n-1, np.int32); fn=np.zeros(n-1, np.int32)
   for d in range(1,n):
      mask=(1 << (n-d)) - 1
      fp[d-1] = (A & (Z << d) & mask).bit_count()
      fn[d-1] = (Z & (A << d) & mask).bit_count()
   return fp,fn
This loops n, building mask each time (1 << (n-d)). Could precompute masks list descending? For n=900, making big ints okay. Optimize:
   shift=1; for d in range(n-1,0,-1)? Maybe. Simpler.
For n=1800, 1<<1800 per loop okay. Could precompute mask for d? Not needed.
But Python bit_count on arbitrary length. Good.

bitset_max_count can reuse similar but no arrays:
def bitset_max_count_from_h(h):
   n=h.size; if n<2 return 0
   A...
   mask_all=...
   Z=...
   m=0
   for d in range(1,n):
      mask=(1 << (n-d))-1
      c=(A & (Z << d) & mask).bit_count()
      if c>m:m=c
      c=(Z & (A << d) & mask).bit_count()
      if c>m:m=c
   return m

def active_scores(h, fp, fn, n):
   if n <= 1: return None
   m2 = 2*(n-1)
   vals = np.empty(m2, dtype=np.int32)
   vals[:n-1]=fp; vals[n-1:]=fn
   maxv = int(vals.max())
   k = min(max(16, n//6), m2)  # e.g., 75 for 450
   if m2 > k:
       idx = np.argpartition(vals, -k)[-k:]
   else: idx=np.arange(m2)
   # ensure if many vals max not included? argpartition includes k high values, okay.
   # maybe to include all equal max if count <k; if count >k arbitrary.
   score=np.zeros(n,float)
   for ii in idx:
       ii=int(ii); d=ii % (n-1); typ=0 if ii < n-1 else 1
       val=int(vals[ii]); if val < maxv-1: maybe skip? But top k may include low if m2<k? m2=900,k=75, top all high. Skip if val < maxv-1 to reduce.
       if val < maxv - 1: continue? But if maxv count <k maybe all maxv; if many max, maxv-1 not selected. Good.
       w=1.0 if val==maxv else 0.3
       # add contributions
       R=n-1-d; L=d
       if typ==0:
          if R>0: score[:R] += w*(1 - h[d:d+R])? h[d:] length R. Yes.
          if L>0: score[L:] -= w*h[:L]
       else:
          if R>0: score[:R] -= w*h[d:]
          if L>0: score[L:] += w*(1 - h[:L])
   z = np.where(h==0)[0]; o=np.where(h==1)[0]
   zcand = z[np.argsort(score[z])][:min(16,z.size)]
   ocand = o[np.argsort(-score[o])][:min(16,o.size)]
   return score, zcand, ocand

Pair evaluate function:
def pair_move_count(h, fp, fn, p, q):
    if h[p] == h[q]: return max(fp.max(),fn.max())
    hp = h.copy()
    if hp[p] == 1 and hp[q] == 0:
        fp2 = fp.copy(); fn2=fn.copy()
        flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, q, 1)
        flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, p, -1)
    else:
        fp2=fp.copy(); fn2=fn.copy()
        flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, p, 1)
        flip_inplace(hp, fp2, fn2, q, -1)
    return int(max(fp2.max(), fn2.max()))

def pair_apply(h, fp, fn, p, q):
    if h[p] == h[q]: return
    if h[p] == 1 and h[q] == 0:
        flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, q, 1)
        flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, p, -1)
    else:
        flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, p, 1)
        flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, q, -1)

In local loop, if best candidate found using pair_move_count, call pair_apply to current. Since pair_move_count did copies, no need store.

Now continuous hill with correct gradient. Let's implement active_continuous_polish:
def active_continuous_polish(h0, time_limit, seed=0, top_k=24):
   h=project_density(h0,0.5*h.size)
   n=h.size; target=0.5*n; h_c=evaluate_erdos(h); best=...
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   start=time.perf_counter(); dl=start+time_limit; stall=0
   for _ in range(5000):
      if time>dl break
      corr=np.correlate(h,1-h)
      center=n-1
      m=corr.max()
      k=min(top_k, corr.size)
      idx=np.argpartition(corr, -k)[-k:]
      g=np.zeros(n)
      # Use weights: softmax over val with temp 0.05? Since corr units integral (0.4), differences small. Use temp=0.02 maybe.
      vals=corr[idx]
      dv=vals - vals.max()
      w=np.exp(dv/0.02)
      w/=w.sum()
      for ii,wt in zip(idx,w):
         if wt < 1e-4: continue
         l = center - int(ii)
         add_corr_grad(g,h,l,float(wt))
      d=-g; d-=np.mean(d)
      norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
      if norm < 1e-14: break
      d/=norm
      improved=False
      # maybe randomize direction sign? no.
      steps=(0.03,0.07,0.15,0.3,0.6)
      for step in steps:
          cand=project_density(h+step*d,target)
          c=evaluate_erdos(cand)
          if c < h_c - 1e-12:
              h=cand; h_c=c; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
          for step in (0.01,0.02):
             cand=...
             if c < h_c -1e-12: accept
      if improved:
          if h_c < best_c: best=...
          stall=0
      else:
          stall+=1
          if stall>40: break
   return best, best_c, n

evaluate_erdos:
def evaluate_erdos(h):
   h=np.asarray(h,float)
   n=h.size
   if n==0: return inf
   dx=2.0/n
   corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
   val=float(np.max(corr))*dx
   return val if np.isfinite(val) else inf

Upsample:
def upsample_h(parent,n):
   if parent.size==n: return parent.copy()
   x0=(arange(parent.size)+0.5)*2/parent.size
   x1=(arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
   h=np.interp(x1,x0,parent,left=parent[0],right=parent[-1])
   return project_density(h,0.5*n)

Bit int:
def binary_to_int(h):
   A=0
   nz=np.nonzero(h)[0]
   for i in nz:
      A |= 1 << int(i)
   return A

compute_f_arrays(h):
   n = int(h.size)
   if n <2: return zeros
   A=binary_to_int(h)
   mask_all=(1<<n)-1
   Z=mask_all ^ A
   fp=np.zeros(n-1,np.int32); fn=...
   for d in range(1,n):
      mask=(1 << (n-d)) -1
      fp[d-1]=(A & (Z << d) & mask).bit_count()
      fn[d-1]=(Z & (A << d) & mask).bit_count()
   return fp,fn

def flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, p, delta):
    n=h.size; d=p if delta? We assume delta ±1.
    R = n - p - 1
    L = p
    if p+1 <= n-1: # R>0
       right = h[p+1:]
    if delta >0:
       if R>0: fp[:R] += (1 - h[p+1:]); fn[:R] -= h[p+1:]
       if L>0:
          left = h[p-1::-1]
          fp[:L] -= left
          fn[:L] += (1 - left)
    else:
       if R>0:
          right = h[p+1:]
          fp[:R] -= (1 - right)
          fn[:R] += right
       if L>0:
          left = h[p-1::-1]
          fp[:L] += left
          fn[:L] -= (1 - left)
    h[p] = int(h[p] + delta)

active_scores: Use int arrays. When h[d:] int8, (1-h[d:]) int8. score float.
Implementation:
def active_scores(h, fp, fn, n, max_lags=96, top_cand=14):
    m2=2*(n-1)
    if m2<=0: return None,None,None
    vals = np.empty(m2, dtype=np.int32)
    vals[:n-1] = fp; vals[n-1:] = fn
    maxv = int(vals.max())
    k = min(max_lags, m2)
    if k < m2:
       idx = np.argpartition(vals, -k)[-k:]
    else:
       idx = np.arange(m2)
    score = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for ii in idx:
       ii=int(ii)
       val=int(vals[ii])
       if val < maxv - 1:
           continue
       w = 1.0 if val == maxv else 0.3
       d = ii % (n-1)
       typ = 0 if ii < (n-1) else 1
       R = n-1-d
       L = d
       if typ == 0:
           if R>0: score[:R] += w * (1 - h[d:])
           if L>0: score[L:] -= w * h[:L]
       else:
           if R>0: score[:R] -= w * h[d:]
           if L>0: score[L:] += w * (1 - h[:L])
    z_idx = np.where(h == 0)[0]
    o_idx = np.where(h == 1)[0]
    if z_idx.size and o_idx.size:
       zcand = z_idx[np.argsort(score[z_idx])[:top_cand]]
       ocand = o_idx[np.argsort(-score[o_idx])[:top_cand]]
    else:
       zcand=np.array([],int); ocand=np.array([],int)
    return score, zcand, ocand

def binary_local_search(h0, fp0, fn0, time_limit, seed=0):

    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.perf_counter(); deadline=start+time_limit
    no_imp=0; best_no=0
    div_mode=0

    max_div = 5 if n<=600 else 2

       if deadline - time.perf_counter() < 0.05? break.

              nc = pair_move_count(h,fp,fn,p,q)

                 pair_apply(h,fp,fn,p,q); cur_c=nc; found=True; break
           if not found:

                 if p==q: continue
                 nc=pair_move_count(...)
                 if nc < cur_c: apply; cur_c=nc; found=True; break
       else: break
       if found:

          else:

                       p=int(rng.choice(zcand)); q=int(rng.choice(ocand))
                   else:
                       p=int(rng.integers(n)); q=int(rng.integers(n))
                   if h[p] != h[q]:
                      nc=pair_move_count(h,fp,fn,p,q)
                      if nc <= cur_c + kick:
                         pair_apply(...); cur_c=nc; div=True; break
             if div:
                no_imp=0

             if no_imp > 60 or best_no > 200: break
       if cur_c > best_c + max(4, n//40):
          h=best_h.copy(); fp,fn=compute_f_arrays(h); cur_c=best_c
    return best_h, int(best_c), n

def continuous_active_polish(h0, time_limit, top_lags=24, seed=0):
   h=project_density(h0,0.5*h0.size); n; h_c=evaluate; best=...
   deadline...
   st=0
   for _ in range(5000):
       if time>deadline break
       corr=np.correlate(h,1-h)
       center=n-1; total=corr.size
       k=min(top_lags,total)
       idx=np.argpartition(corr,-k)[-k:] if k<total else np.arange(total)
       vals=corr[idx]
       dv=vals-vals.max()
       temp=0.01
       w=np.exp(np.clip(dv/temp,-745,0)); s=w.sum(); if s<=0: break else w/=s
       g=np.zeros(n)
       for ii,wt in zip(idx,w):
           if wt<1e-4: continue
           l=center-int(ii)
           add_corr_grad(g,h,l,float(wt))
       d=-g; d-=np.mean(d); norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if norm<1e-14: break
       d/=norm
       improved=False
       for step in (0.03,0.08,0.18,0.35,0.7):
           cand=project_density(h+step*d, target)
           c=evaluate_erdos(cand)

              h=cand; h_c=c; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           for step in (0.01,0.02): ...
       if improved:
          st=0
          if h_c<best_c: best...
       else:
          st+=1
          if st>30: break
   return best,best_c

Top-level smooth functions:
def _sm_objective(x):
    h=np.asarray(x,float)
    n=_SM_N
    if n<=1: return 0.0
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    m=float(np.max(corr))
    y=_SM_ALPHA*(corr-m)
    y=np.clip(y,-745,0)
    e=np.exp(y)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return 1e100
    return float(_SM_DX*(m + math.log(s)/_SM_ALPHA))
def _sm_gradient(x):
    h=np.asarray(x,float); n=_SM_N
    if n<=1: return zeros
    corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    m=np.max(corr)
    y=_SM_ALPHA*(corr-m); y=clip; e=exp; s=e.sum(); if s<=0 return zeros
    w=e/s; g=zeros
    center=n-1
    for i in range(corr.size):
       wt=w[i]
       if wt<1e-6: continue
       l=center-i
       add_corr_grad(g,h,l,wt)
    return _SM_DX*g
def _sm_sum(x): return float(np.sum(x)) - _SM_TARGET
def _sm_sum_jac(x): return np.ones_like(x)

SLSQP call:
def slsqp_smooth_polish(h0, time_limit, seed=0):
   global _SM_N,_SM_ALPHA,_SM_DX,_SM_TARGET
   h0=project_density(h0,0.5*h0.size); n=h0.size; target=0.5*n
   _SM_N=n; _SM_DX=2.0/n; _SM_TARGET=target
   best=...
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   cons=({"type":"eq","fun":_sm_sum,"jac":_sm_sum_jac},)
   for alpha in (20,60,150):
      if time>deadline break
      _SM_ALPHA=alpha
      maxiter = 60 if n<=300 else 30
      try:
         res=minimize(_sm_objective,h,jac=_sm_gradient,bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,method='SLSQP',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-10,'disp':False})
         if res.x...:
            cand=project_density(res.x,target); c=eval; if c<best: best...
            h = cand if c < best+0.02 else h
      except: pass
   return best, best_c

def allocate_counts(fr,total):
   fr=...; fr=np.clip(fr,0,None); s=fr.sum(); if s<=0: fr=np.ones(len)/len else fr/=s
   raw=fr*float(total)
   base=np.floor(raw).astype(int)
   rem=raw-base
   diff=int(total - base.sum())
   if diff>0:
      order=np.argsort(-rem)
      for i in range(diff):
          base[order[i % base.size]] += 1
   elif diff<0:
      order=np.argsort(rem)
      removed=0
      for i in range(max(0,-diff)*3):
          if removed >= -diff: break
          j=order[i % base.size]
          if base[j] > 0:
             base[j]-=1; removed+=1
      if removed < -diff:
          # fallback: add to largest to fix? Actually we removed too few because zeros, base.sum > total; add? Let's recompute diff.
          diff=int(total - base.sum())
          if diff>0:
             order=np.argsort(-rem)
             for i in range(diff): base[order[i%base.size]] +=1
   # final ensure sum exactly
   diff=int(total - base.sum())
   if diff>0:
      order=np.argsort(-rem) ...
   elif diff<0:
      # remove from largest base to avoid zeros
      order=np.argsort(-base)
      for i in range(-diff): base[order[i%base.size]] -=1
   return base
This should handle.

def add_corr_grad(g,h,l,w):
   n=h.size; d=abs(l); R=n-d
   if d==0: g += w*(1-2*h)
   elif l>0:
      g[:R] += w*(1.0 - h[d:d+R])
      g[d:d+R] -= w*h[:R]
   else:
      g[d:d+R] += w*(1.0 - h[:R])
      g[:R] -= w*h[d:d+R]

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


_GLOBAL_BLOCK = None
_SM_N = 1
_SM_ALPHA = 1.0
_SM_DX = 1.0
_SM_TARGET = 0.0


def project_density(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x.copy()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(target)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-12:
        return x
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    res = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(res) > 1e-10:
        free = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if free.size:
            h[free] += res / float(free.size)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
            res = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(res) > 1e-10:
            free2 = np.where((h > 0.0) & (h < 1.0))[0]
            if free2.size:
                free2 = free2[np.argsort(np.abs(h[free2] - 0.5), kind="mergesort")]
                j = int(free2[0])
                room = 1.0 - h[j] if res > 0 else h[j]
                h[j] = np.clip(h[j] + np.sign(res) * min(abs(res), abs(room)), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def evaluate_erdos(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if corr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    val = float(np.max(corr)) * dx
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def upsample_h(parent, n):
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
    n0 = p.size
    if n0 == 0:
        return project_density(np.full(max(1, n), 0.5), 0.5 * n)
    if n0 == n:
        return p.copy()
    x0 = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n0))
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = np.interp(x1, x0, p, left=float(p[0]), right=float(p[-1]))
    return project_density(h, 0.5 * n)


def binary_from_fractional(hs):
    h = np.asarray(hs, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n % 2 != 0:
        h = upsample_h(h, n + 1)
        n = h.size
    target = n // 2
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    order = np.argsort(h, kind="mergesort")
    if target <= 0:
        return out
    if target >= n:
        out[:] = 1
        return out
    out[order[target:]] = 1
    return out


def binary_to_int(h):
    A = 0
    nz = np.nonzero(h)[0]
    for i in nz:
        A |= 1 << int(i)
    return A


def bitset_max_count(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int8)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n < 2:
        return 0
    A = binary_to_int(h)
    mask_all = (1 << n) - 1
    Z = mask_all ^ A
    best = 0
    for d in range(1, n):
        mask = (1 << (n - d)) - 1
        c1 = (A & (Z >> d) & mask).bit_count()
        if c1 > best:
            best = c1
        c0 = (Z & (A >> d) & mask).bit_count()
        if c0 > best:
            best = c0
    return int(best)


def compute_f_arrays(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int8)
    n = int(h.size)
    fp = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.int32)
    fn = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.int32)
    if n < 2:
        return fp, fn
    A = binary_to_int(h)
    mask_all = (1 << n) - 1
    Z = mask_all ^ A
    for d in range(1, n):
        mask = (1 << (n - d)) - 1
        fp[d - 1] = (A & (Z >> d) & mask).bit_count()
        fn[d - 1] = (Z & (A >> d) & mask).bit_count()
    return fp, fn


def flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, p, delta):
    n = h.size
    R = n - p - 1
    L = p
    if delta > 0:
        if R > 0:
            right = h[p + 1:p + 1 + R]
            fp[:R] += (1 - right)
            fn[:R] -= right
        if L > 0:
            left = h[p - 1::-1]
            fp[:L] -= left
            fn[:L] += (1 - left)
    else:
        if R > 0:
            right = h[p + 1:p + 1 + R]
            fp[:R] -= (1 - right)
            fn[:R] += right
        if L > 0:
            left = h[p - 1::-1]
            fp[:L] += left
            fn[:L] -= (1 - left)
    h[p] = int(h[p] + delta)


def pair_apply(h, fp, fn, p, q):
    if h[p] == h[q]:
        return
    if h[p] == 1 and h[q] == 0:
        flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, q, 1)
        flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, p, -1)
    else:
        flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, p, 1)
        flip_inplace(h, fp, fn, q, -1)


def pair_move_count(h, fp, fn, p, q):
    if h[p] == h[q]:
        return int(max(fp.max(), fn.max()))
    hp = h.copy()
    fp2 = fp.copy()
    fn2 = fn.copy()
    pair_apply(hp, fp2, fn2, p, q)
    return int(max(fp2.max(), fn2.max()))


def active_scores(h, fp, fn, n, max_lags=96, top_cand=14):
    m2 = 2 * (n - 1)
    if m2 <= 0:
        return None, np.array([], dtype=int), np.array([], dtype=int)
    vals = np.empty(m2, dtype=np.int32)
    vals[:n - 1] = fp
    vals[n - 1:] = fn
    maxv = int(vals.max())
    k = min(max_lags, m2)
    if k < m2:
        idx = np.argpartition(vals, -k)[-k:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(m2)
    score = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    half = n - 1
    for ii in idx:
        ii = int(ii)
        val = int(vals[ii])
        if val < maxv - 1:
            continue
        w = 1.0 if val == maxv else 0.3
        d = ii % half
        typ = 0 if ii < half else 1
        R = n - d
        if R <= 0:
            continue
        if typ == 0:
            score[:R] += w * (1 - h[d:d + R])
            score[d:d + R] -= w * h[:R]
        else:
            score[:R] -= w * h[d:d + R]
            score[d:d + R] += w * (1 - h[:R])
    z_idx = np.where(h == 0)[0]
    o_idx = np.where(h == 1)[0]
    if z_idx.size:
        zcand = z_idx[np.argsort(score[z_idx], kind="mergesort")[:top_cand]]
    else:
        zcand = np.array([], dtype=int)
    if o_idx.size:
        ocand = o_idx[np.argsort(-score[o_idx], kind="mergesort")[:top_cand]]
    else:
        ocand = np.array([], dtype=int)
    return score, zcand, ocand


def binary_local_search(h0, fp0, fn0, time_limit, seed=0):
    n = int(np.asarray(h0).size)
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 0:
        h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=np.int8)
        c = int(max(np.asarray(fp0).max(), np.asarray(fn0).max()))
        return h0, c, n
    h = np.asarray(h0, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    fp = np.asarray(fp0, dtype=np.int32).copy()
    fn = np.asarray(fn0, dtype=np.int32).copy()
    cur_c = int(max(fp.max(), fn.max()))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_fp = fp.copy()
    best_fn = fn.copy()
    best_c = cur_c
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.perf_counter()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)
    no_imp = 0
    best_no = 0
    max_trials = 90 if n <= 600 else 45
    max_rand = max(6, max_trials // 8)
    max_kicks = 8 if n <= 600 else 3

    while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
        if deadline - time.perf_counter() < 0.05:
            break
        score, zcand, ocand = active_scores(h, fp, fn, n, max_lags=(120 if n <= 600 else 60), top_cand=14)
        found = False
        if zcand.size and ocand.size:
            pairs = []
            for p in zcand:
                p = int(p)
                sp = float(score[p])
                for q in ocand:
                    q = int(q)
                    if p != q:
                        pairs.append((sp - float(score[q]), p, q))
            pairs.sort()
            for _est, p, q in pairs[:max_trials]:
                nc = pair_move_count(h, fp, fn, p, q)
                if nc < cur_c:
                    pair_apply(h, fp, fn, p, q)
                    cur_c = nc
                    found = True
                    break
            if not found:
                for _ in range(max_rand):
                    p = int(rng.choice(zcand))
                    q = int(rng.choice(ocand))
                    if p == q:
                        continue
                    nc = pair_move_count(h, fp, fn, p, q)
                    if nc < cur_c:
                        pair_apply(h, fp, fn, p, q)
                        cur_c = nc
                        found = True
                        break
        if found:
            no_imp = 0
            if cur_c < best_c:
                best_c = cur_c
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_fp = fp.copy()
                best_fn = fn.copy()
                best_no = 0
            else:
                best_no += 1
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            best_no += 1
            kick = 0
            if best_no >= 25:
                kick = 1
            if best_no >= 80:
                kick = 2
            kicked = False
            if kick > 0 and zcand.size and ocand.size:
                for _ in range(max_kicks * 3):
                    if rng.random() < 0.75:
                        p = int(rng.choice(zcand))
                        q = int(rng.choice(ocand))
                    else:
                        p = int(rng.integers(n))
                        q = int(rng.integers(n))
                    if h[p] == h[q]:
                        continue
                    nc = pair_move_count(h, fp, fn, p, q)
                    if nc <= cur_c + kick:
                        pair_apply(h, fp, fn, p, q)
                        cur_c = nc
                        kicked = True
                        break
            if kicked:
                no_imp = 0
            if cur_c > best_c + max(4, n // 35):
                h = best_h.copy()
                fp = best_fp.copy()
                fn = best_fn.copy()
                cur_c = best_c
            if no_imp > 70 or best_no > 240:
                break
    return best_h, int(best_c), n


def add_corr_grad(g, h, lag, w):
    n = h.size
    d = abs(int(lag))
    if d == 0:
        g += float(w) * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        return
    R = n - d
    if R <= 0:
        return
    w = float(w)
    if lag > 0:
        g[:R] += w * (1.0 - h[d:d + R])
        g[d:d + R] -= w * h[:R]
    else:
        g[d:d + R] += w * (1.0 - h[:R])
        g[:R] -= w * h[d:d + R]


def continuous_active_polish(h0, time_limit, top_lags=24, seed=0):
    h = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 0:
        c = evaluate_erdos(h)
        return h, c
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_density(h, target)
    h_c = evaluate_erdos(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = h_c
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.perf_counter()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)
    stall = 0

    while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
        if deadline - time.perf_counter() < 0.1:
            break
        corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        center = n - 1
        total = corr.size
        k = min(max(4, int(top_lags)), total)
        if k < total:
            idx = np.argpartition(corr, -k)[-k:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(total)
        vals = corr[idx]
        dv = vals - vals.max()
        temp = 0.015
        w = np.exp(np.clip(dv / temp, -745.0, 0.0))
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            break
        w = w / s
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for ii, wt in zip(idx, w):
            if wt < 1e-4:
                continue
            lag = center - int(ii)
            add_corr_grad(g, h, lag, float(wt))
        d = -g
        d -= np.mean(d)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if norm < 1e-14:
            break
        d /= norm
        improved = False
        steps = (0.025, 0.06, 0.12, 0.25, 0.5, 0.9)
        for step in steps:
            cand = project_density(h + step * d, target)
            c = evaluate_erdos(cand)
            if c < h_c - 1e-12:
                h = cand
                h_c = c
                improved = True
                break
        if not improved:
            for step in (0.008, 0.016):
                cand = project_density(h + step * d, target)
                c = evaluate_erdos(cand)
                if c < h_c - 1e-12:
                    h = cand
                    h_c = c
                    improved = True
                    break
        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if h_c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = h_c
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 45:
                break
    return best_h, best_c


def _sm_objective(x):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = _SM_N
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if corr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    y = _SM_ALPHA * (corr - m)
    y = np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    return float(_SM_DX * (m + math.log(s) / _SM_ALPHA))


def _sm_gradient(x):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = _SM_N
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(max(1, n), dtype=float)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if corr.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    y = _SM_ALPHA * (corr - m)
    y = np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w = e / s
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    for i, wt in enumerate(w):
        if wt < 1e-6:
            continue
        lag = center - int(i)
        add_corr_grad(g, h, lag, float(wt))
    return _SM_DX * g


def _sm_sum(x):
    return float(np.sum(x)) - _SM_TARGET


def _sm_sum_jac(x):
    return np.ones_like(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), dtype=float)


def slsqp_smooth_polish(h0, time_limit, seed=0):
    global _SM_N, _SM_ALPHA, _SM_DX, _SM_TARGET
    h = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 0:
        return h, evaluate_erdos(h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_density(h, target)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = evaluate_erdos(h)
    _SM_N = n
    _SM_DX = 2.0 / float(n)
    _SM_TARGET = target
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = ({"type": "eq", "fun": _sm_sum, "jac": _sm_sum_jac},)
    start = time.perf_counter()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)
    alphas = (25.0, 70.0, 180.0)
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.perf_counter() >= deadline - 8.0:
            break
        _SM_ALPHA = alpha
        maxiter = 70 if n <= 300 else (40 if n <= 700 else 25)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _sm_objective,
                h,
                method="SLSQP",
                jac=_sm_gradient,
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-10, "disp": False}
            )
            if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                cand = project_density(res.x, target)
                c = evaluate_erdos(cand)
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    best_h = cand
                h = cand if c < best_c + 0.025 else h
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_h, best_c


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=float)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = x - np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(x, -745.0, 709.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float) / float(x.size)
    return e / s


def _interval_overlap_int(I, J, k):
    s = 0.0
    for l1, r1 in I:
        for l0, r0 in J:
            lo = max(l1, l0 - k)
            hi = min(r1, r0 - k)
            if hi > lo:
                s += hi - lo
    return s


def _block_obj_fracs(a, b, start):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return 1.0
    sa = float(np.sum(a))
    sb = float(np.sum(b))
    if sa <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=float)
    else:
        a = a / sa
    if sb <= 0.0:
        b = np.ones_like(b, dtype=float)
    else:
        b = b / sb
    p = a.size
    q = b.size
    pos = 0.0
    ones = []
    zeros = []
    bounds = [0.0]
    val = 1 if int(start) == 1 else 0
    i1 = 0
    i0 = 0
    for _ in range(p + q):
        if val == 1:
            length = float(a[i1])
            l = pos
            r = pos + length
            ones.append((l, r))
            i1 += 1
        else:
            length = float(b[i0])
            l = pos
            r = pos + length
            zeros.append((l, r))
            i0 += 1
        pos += length
        bounds.append(pos)
        val = 1 - val
    pts = set()
    for u in bounds:
        for v in bounds:
            d = abs(float(u) - float(v))
            if d <= 2.0 + 1e-12:
                pts.add(min(2.0, d))
    pts.add(0.0)
    pts.add(2.0)
    best = 0.0
    for k in pts:
        if k < -1e-12 or k > 2.0 + 1e-12:
            continue
        v1 = _interval_overlap_int(ones, zeros, float(k))
        if v1 > best:
            best = v1
        v0 = _interval_overlap_int(zeros, ones, float(k))
        if v0 > best:
            best = v0
    return float(best)


def _block_wrapper(x):
    pat = _GLOBAL_BLOCK
    if pat is None:
        return 1e100
    p, q, start = pat
    a = _softmax(x[:p])
    b = _softmax(x[p:p + q])
    return _block_obj_fracs(a, b, start)


def optimize_block_patterns(time_limit, seed=42):
    global _GLOBAL_BLOCK
    res_list = []
    if time_limit <= 1:
        return res_list
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.perf_counter()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)
    for m in range(3, 9):
        if time.perf_counter() > deadline - 5:
            break
        for st in (1, 0):
            if time.perf_counter() > deadline - 5:
                break
            p = (m + 1) // 2 if st == 1 else m // 2
            q = m - p
            if p < 1 or q < 1:
                continue
            dim = (p - 1) + (q - 1)
            nstarts = 5 if dim <= 3 else 3
            best_val = 1e100
            best_x = None
            for s in range(nstarts):
                if time.perf_counter() > deadline - 5:
                    break
                if s == 0:
                    x0 = np.zeros(p + q, dtype=float)
                else:
                    x0 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, p + q)
                _GLOBAL_BLOCK = (p, q, st)
                try:
                    res = minimize(
                        _block_wrapper,
                        x0,
                        method="Nelder-Mead",
                        options={"maxiter": 220, "maxfev": 350,
                                 "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-8, "adaptive": True}
                    )
                    if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                        val = _block_wrapper(res.x)
                        if val < best_val:
                            best_val = float(val)
                            best_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).copy()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if best_x is not None:
                a = _softmax(best_x[:p])
                b = _softmax(best_x[p:p + q])
                res_list.append((best_val, p, q, st, a, b))
    return res_list


def allocate_counts(fr, total):
    fr = np.asarray(fr, dtype=float)
    fr = np.nan_to_num(fr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    fr = np.clip(fr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(fr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        fr = np.ones_like(fr, dtype=float) / float(fr.size)
    else:
        fr = fr / s
    total = int(total)
    raw = fr * float(total)
    base = np.floor(raw).astype(int)
    rem = raw - base
    diff = int(total - float(base.sum()))
    if diff > 0:
        order = np.argsort(-rem, kind="mergesort")
        for i in range(diff):
            base[int(order[i % base.size])] += 1
    elif diff < 0:
        need = -diff
        order = np.argsort(rem, kind="mergesort")
        removed = 0
        for i in range(need * max(1, base.size)):
            if removed >= need:
                break
            j = int(order[i % base.size])
            if base[j] > 0:
                base[j] -= 1
                removed += 1
        diff = int(total - float(base.sum()))
        if diff > 0:
            order = np.argsort(-rem, kind="mergesort")
            for i in range(diff):
                base[int(order[i % base.size])] += 1
        elif diff < 0:
            order = np.argsort(-base, kind="mergesort")
            for i in range(-diff):
                base[int(order[i % base.size])] -= 1
    diff = int(total - float(base.sum()))
    if diff > 0:
        order = np.argsort(-rem, kind="mergesort")
        for i in range(diff):
            base[int(order[i % base.size])] += 1
    elif diff < 0:
        order = np.argsort(-base, kind="mergesort")
        for i in range(-diff):
            base[int(order[i % base.size])] -= 1
    return base


def make_block_candidate(n, start, a, b):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    target = n // 2
    ones_counts = allocate_counts(a, target)
    zeros_counts = allocate_counts(b, target)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    pos = 0
    val = 1 if int(start) == 1 else 0
    i1 = 0
    i0 = 0
    m = int(a.size + b.size)
    for _ in range(m):
        if val == 1:
            c = int(ones_counts[i1]) if i1 < int(ones_counts.size) else 0
            i1 += 1
        else:
            c = int(zeros_counts[i0]) if i0 < int(zeros_counts.size) else 0
            i0 += 1
        if c > 0 and pos + c <= n:
            h[pos:pos + c] = val
            pos += c
        val = 1 - val
    diff = target - int(h.sum())
    if diff > 0:
        idx = np.where(h == 0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[idx[:diff]] = 1
    elif diff < 0:
        idx = np.where(h == 1)[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[idx[: -diff]] = 0
    return h


def make_generic_start(n, mode, rng):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    if mode == 0:
        h = 0.5 + 0.32 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * x / 2.0 + rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28))
        h += 0.14 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 5.0 * x / 2.0 + rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28))
    elif mode == 1:
        h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        seg = max(1, int(n // 8))
        for i in range(0, n, seg):
            end = min(i + seg, n)
            h[i:end] = rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9)
    elif mode == 2:
        u = rng.random(n)
        med = float(np.median(u))
        h = np.where(u > med, 0.72, 0.28).astype(float)
        h += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
    else:
        h = 0.5 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * int(rng.integers(2, 7)) * x / 2.0 + rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28))
    return project_density(h, 0.5 * n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1300.0
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget - 18.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0
    best_bin = None
    best_bin_c = float("inf")
    cont_start = None

    parent = None
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if raw.ndim == 1 and raw.size >= 2:
            parent = np.nan_to_num(raw, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
            parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    bin_cands = []

    if parent is not None:
        pn = int(parent.size)
        pn_even = pn if pn % 2 == 0 else pn + 1
        parr = upsample_h(parent, pn_even)
        cp = evaluate_erdos(parr)
        best_h = parr.copy()
        best_c = cp
        best_n = pn_even
        cont_start = parr.copy()
        hbin = binary_from_fractional(parr)
        mc = bitset_max_count(hbin)
        cb = float(2 * mc) / float(hbin.size)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_h = hbin.astype(float)
            best_c = cb
            best_n = hbin.size
        best_bin = hbin
        best_bin_c = cb
        bin_cands.append((cb, pn_even, hbin))
    else:
        n0 = 450
        x = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n0))
        h_init = 0.5 + 0.30 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * x / 2.0) + 0.15 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 5.0 * x / 2.0 + 1.0)
        h_init = project_density(h_init, 0.5 * n0)
        best_h = h_init.copy()
        best_c = evaluate_erdos(h_init)
        best_n = n0
        cont_start = h_init.copy()
        hbin = binary_from_fractional(h_init)
        mc = bitset_max_count(hbin)
        cb = float(2 * mc) / float(hbin.size)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_h = hbin.astype(float)
            best_c = cb
            best_n = hbin.size
        best_bin = hbin
        best_bin_c = cb
        bin_cands.append((cb, n0, hbin))

    now = time.perf_counter()
    if deadline - now > 70.0:
        block_time = min(75.0, max(10.0, 0.10 * budget))
        patterns = optimize_block_patterns(block_time, seed + 1)
        n_options = []
        for nn in (450, 600, 900):
            if nn not in n_options:
                n_options.append(nn)
        if parent is not None:
            pe = int(parent.size)
            if pe % 2 != 0:
                pe += 1
            if 180 <= pe <= 1500 and pe not in n_options:
                n_options.append(pe)
        n_options = sorted(n_options)[:4]
        for _val, _p, _q, st, a, b in patterns:
            now = time.perf_counter()
            if now > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            for nn in n_options:
                now = time.perf_counter()
                if now > deadline - 3.0:
                    break
                try:
                    h = make_block_candidate(nn, st, a, b)
                    mc = bitset_max_count(h)
                    c = float(2 * mc) / float(nn)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if c < best_c:
                    best_h = h.astype(float)
                    best_c = c
                    best_n = nn
                if c < best_bin_c:
                    best_bin = h
                    best_bin_c = c
                dup = False
                for cc, nc, hc in bin_cands:
                    if nc == nn and np.array_equal(hc, h):
                        dup = True
                        break
                if not dup:
                    bin_cands.append((c, nn, h))

    if bin_cands:
        cs = np.array([x[0] for x in bin_cands], dtype=float)
        order = np.argsort(cs, kind="mergesort")
        top = []
        for idx in order:
            c, n, h = bin_cands[int(idx)]
            dup = False
            for _c, _n, _h in top:
                if _n == n and np.array_equal(_h, h):
                    dup = True
                    break
            if not dup:
                top.append(bin_cands[int(idx)])
            if len(top) >= 5:
                break
    else:
        top = []

    now = time.perf_counter()
    if deadline - now > 80.0:
        local_total = min(700.0, max(60.0, 0.55 * (deadline - now)))
        top = top[:4]
        if best_bin is not None:
            have = False
            for _c, n, h in top:
                if n == best_bin.size and np.array_equal(h, best_bin):
                    have = True
                    break
            if not have:
                top.append((best_bin_c, best_bin.size, best_bin))
        weights = (1.0, 0.55, 0.30, 0.16)
        ww = 0.0
        for i in range(min(len(top), 4)):
            ww += weights[i]
        if ww > 0.0:
            for i in range(min(len(top), 4)):
                now = time.perf_counter()
                if now > deadline - 35.0:
                    break
                c0, n0, h0 = top[i]
                if i > 0 and c0 > best_c + 0.02:
                    break
                share = local_total * weights[i] / ww
                share = max(12.0, min(share, deadline - now - 25.0))
                if share <= 2.0:
                    break
                try:
                    fp, fn = compute_f_arrays(h0)
                    hres, cres, nres = binary_local_search(h0, fp, fn, share, seed + 17 + i)
                    crow = float(2 * cres) / float(nres)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if crow < best_c:
                    best_c = crow
                    best_h = hres.astype(float)
                    best_n = nres
                if crow < best_bin_c:
                    best_bin_c = crow
                    best_bin = hres.copy()

    now = time.perf_counter()
    if best_bin is not None and deadline - now > 35.0:
        try:
            fp, fn = compute_f_arrays(best_bin)
            t2 = min(50.0, deadline - now - 12.0)
            hres, cres, nres = binary_local_search(best_bin, fp, fn, t2, seed + 129)
            crow = float(2 * cres) / float(nres)
            if crow < best_c:
                best_c = crow
                best_h = hres.astype(float)
                best_n = nres
            if crow < best_bin_c:
                best_bin_c = crow
                best_bin = hres.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    now = time.perf_counter()
    if deadline - now > 90.0:
        cont_starts = []
        if cont_start is not None:
            cont_starts.append(cont_start)
        if best_h is not None:
            bh = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
            if not np.all((bh == 0) | (bh == 1)):
                if not any(np.allclose(x, bh, atol=1e-8) for x in cont_starts):
                    cont_starts.append(bh)
        if best_bin is not None and len(cont_starts) < 3:
            hb = best_bin.astype(float)
            hsm = project_density(hb + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, hb.size), 0.5 * hb.size)
            cont_starts.append(hsm)
        if cont_starts:
            cont_budget = min(360.0, max(30.0, 0.35 * (deadline - now)))
            per = cont_budget / float(min(len(cont_starts), 3))
            for si, hstart in enumerate(cont_starts[:3]):
                now = time.perf_counter()
                if now > deadline - 20.0:
                    break
                try:
                    hcur = project_density(hstart, 0.5 * hstart.size)
                    hcur = project_density(hcur, 0.5 * hcur.size)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                ccur = evaluate_erdos(hcur)
                if ccur < best_c:
                    best_c = ccur
                    best_h = hcur
                    best_n = hcur.size
                for tl in (14, 36):
                    now = time.perf_counter()
                    rem = deadline - now - 12.0
                    if rem < 12.0:
                        break
                    tshare = min(per * 0.55, rem * 0.55)
                    if tshare < 8.0:
                        break
                    try:
                        hpol, cpol = continuous_active_polish(hcur, tshare, top_lags=tl, seed=seed + 200 + si + tl)
                    except Exception:
                        break
                    if cpol < best_c:
                        best_c = cpol
                        best_h = hpol
                        best_n = hpol.size
                    hcur = hpol
                    ccur = cpol
                now = time.perf_counter()
                rem = deadline - now - 15.0
                if rem > 35.0 and hcur.size <= 650:
                    try:
                        hsl, csl = slsqp_smooth_polish(hcur, min(55.0, rem * 0.55), seed + 300 + si)
                        if csl < best_c:
                            best_c = csl
                            best_h = hsl
                            best_n = hsl.size
                        hcur = hsl
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                try:
                    hbin2 = binary_from_fractional(hcur)
                    mc2 = bitset_max_count(hbin2)
                    c2 = float(2 * mc2) / float(hbin2.size)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_c = c2
                    best_h = hbin2.astype(float)
                    best_n = hbin2.size
                if c2 < best_bin_c:
                    best_bin_c = c2
                    best_bin = hbin2.copy()

    now = time.perf_counter()
    if best_bin is not None and deadline - now > 20.0:
        try:
            fp, fn = compute_f_arrays(best_bin)
            hres, cres, nres = binary_local_search(best_bin, fp, fn, max(5.0, deadline - now - 5.0), seed + 777)
            crow = float(2 * cres) / float(nres)
            if crow < best_c:
                best_c = crow
                best_h = hres.astype(float)
                best_n = nres
            if crow < best_bin_c:
                best_bin_c = crow
                best_bin = hres.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is None:
        h = project_density(np.full(450, 0.5, dtype=float), 225.0)
        best_h = h
        best_c = evaluate_erdos(h)
        best_n = 450

    h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        target = float(n) * 0.5
        if int(h.sum()) == int(target) and np.all((h == 0.0) | (h == 1.0)):
            pass
        else:
            h = project_density(h, target)
    else:
        h = project_density(h, 0.5 * n)
    final_c = evaluate_erdos(h)
    return h, float(final_c), int(h.size)
```
